# (s3c-ann) and (s3c-ann-net): the settlement terms of (s3c) read at leads 2 to 5 from the auction announcement (#1288)

**Does letting (s3c) see a settlement that was announced days earlier help at leads 2 to 5?** On the 2026 development days, grid CRPS against (s3c): (s3c-ann) +2.4% [+0.0%, +4.3%]; d +0.042 [+0.011, +0.068]: **gain**; (s3c-ann-net) +4.8% [+1.4%, +7.0%]; d +0.084 [+0.052, +0.112]: **gain**; on the development window, grid CRPS against (s3c): (s3c-ann) +1.6% [+0.4%, +2.8%]; d +0.027 [+0.009, +0.044]: **gain**; (s3c-ann-net) +1.7% [+1.0%, +2.5%]; d +0.028 [+0.016, +0.042]: **gain**.

**Hypothesis and step:** H5 (primary goal), step 3, an arm on the candidate (s3c); ledger item 71 on #1116, avenue #1286. **Development data, not a verdict** (scoring protocol v1, #640). Her ruling of 28 October 2026 (chat, relayed on #1288): test avenue #1286. **(s3c) stays the candidate until she rules on this result.** Nothing is registered or published and no claim changes: `metadata/sources.json`, the published declaration, `metadata/settlement_announcement_dating.json` and `docs/decisions/information-set.md` are unchanged, and the announcement dating stays off in every published declaration. Generated by `scripts/s3c_announced_settlement_1288.py page` from `docs/research/s3c-announced-settlement-1288/result.json`, not edited by hand; declared in `metadata/s3c_announced_settlement_1288.json` before any score.

**What changed.** (s3c)'s two settlement terms are scheduled fields of the target row and are public at 15:00 on the panel day before it settles (`metadata/sources.json`), so (s3c) reads them at lead 1 only. The arms read them at leads 2 to 5 too, dated from the auction announcement (12:00 New York time on the `announcemt_date`, #541, ruled by Eleonora on 10 October 2026 and not in force): at lead h the term of target day T is read from what had been announced by 16:00 on the panel day h rows before T. **(s3c-ann)** keeps the two 0/1 flags (1 when the announced offerings of the leg settling on T are above zero). **(s3c-ann-net)** sizes the two terms by #1273's net settlement (the offerings less the announced publicly held maturing amount, in units of USD 100 bn, the sign kept; a class announced only in part reads 0 until fully announced). Everything else is (s3)'s and the SRF cap is (s3c)'s. At lead 1 the arms are (s3c) and (s3c-net) cell for cell (checked for every scored decision day), so any difference sits at h = 2 to 5. **The legs are reported separately** by two reference walks, not arms: (s3c-ann-coupon) announces the coupon flag alone and (s3c-ann-bill) the bill flag alone.

**The bill leg carries Lens E's caveat** (`docs/reviews/2026-10-19-five-lens/lens-E-data-plumbing.md`, item G). Treasury publishes no clock time for an announcement, and the 12:00 announcement time of a cash management bill is unverified: a cash management bill is ad hoc, announced the same or the previous day, sometimes auctioned at 11:30 and settled at T+0 or T+1. If one is announced after 12:00 the arm counts it too early, and the bill leg's gain at h = 2 to 5 would be read from information a desk did not yet have. An announcement dated before the decision day counts whatever its clock time; one dated the decision day itself counts only if it was made by 16:00, which the 12:00 stamp assumes (any stamp from 12:00 to 15:59 gives the same sums). The page does not measure how many flags hang on a same-day announcement. The settlement-day-type tables below separate the bill settlement days with a cash management bill from those without one.

Panel digest `4ddc3882cd6d406b11e1088f8ff6195e16dde175a0ad6818abe40dd5bdac8999`. Studentized stationary bootstrap (ruling 1), mean block 10 business days, 10,000 draws, seed 1288, two-sided 90% interval (the lower end is the one-sided 95% bound). Positive mean d favours the arm named first in the table's heading. **A reading (gain, loss, undecided) is taken from the mean-d bound** (her ruling on #1009), with the skill interval beside it; for a Brier score the skill is the Brier skill score. **Caveat (Lens C, #958):** the lag-10 HAC standard error that studentizes the bound is understated about 2x after 2020, so an interval that excludes zero is narrower than it should be. The intervals of a group of target days keep the **current method** (the group's days, scattered, resampled in date order as if consecutive), which #1221 questions and #1228 will decide; no reading of a day-type group is a finding. Where two arms differ on few days of a group, some resamples have no long-run variance: the bound is then not computed, the unstudentized interval of mean d is given beside it, and no reading is taken from that. Groups of fewer than 20 days are listed with their means and not tested. The announced-term splits are tested against (s3c) and (s3c-net) (and (s3c-ann) for the net arm) only; every other split against every reference, the benchmarks included. For length, the page prints the regime, pressure-day-type, month, calendar-day-type and settlement-day-type splits against (s3c) and (s3c-net) (and (s3c-ann) for the net arm) and the benchmark tables for the lead, settlement-day-type, regime and pressure-day-type splits; the other pairings are in the record.

**What kind of look this is.** The 2026 days were already seen by #845, by the #151 final test, by #1229, #1252, #1255 and #1273. This is a check on an arm queued by her ruling, not evidence for a verdict. The announced terms act at h = 2 to 5 only: the pooled figure averages four leads that can differ with one that cannot, so read the lead rows beside it.

## The development window (targets up to 2025-12-31; #1145, #1241)

Scored decision days: 1,855 (2018-07-19 to 2025-12-23); 89 refits of each arm's walk. At lead 1 the announced gross settlement and the panel's gross columns differ on 0 (column, day) pairs of 1,859 scored target days. Cells on which an arm is not (s3c) element for element, by lead (of 1,855 each): (s3c-ann): h=1: 0, h=2: 1,855, h=3: 1,855, h=4: 1,855, h=5: 1,855; (s3c-ann-net): h=1: 1,855, h=2: 1,855, h=3: 1,855, h=4: 1,855, h=5: 1,855.

The effect is the standardised effect of the refit the forecast used times the scale at the anchor in bp. The pooled row is printed at every lead and the regime rows at h = 3 only (the record holds them all). The interval is a bootstrap interval of the mean over decision days, which share refits; it is not a sampling interval for the coefficient. A term enters a fit only when at least three training pairs carry it; the share of days in the fit is shown for the bill and the coupon term (a term of the other leg in a leg-reference walk is not in its fit at h = 2 to 5 and reads 0 there).

Groups of fewer than 20 days, listed and not tested: pressure_day_type/year_end (15).

## The 2026 development days (targets 2026-01-02 to 2026-09-03; #1229, #1252)

Scored decision days: 165 (2025-12-31 to 2026-08-27); 97 refits of each arm's walk. At lead 1 the announced gross settlement and the panel's gross columns differ on 0 (column, day) pairs of 169 scored target days. Cells on which an arm is not (s3c) element for element, by lead (of 165 each): (s3c-ann): h=1: 0, h=2: 165, h=3: 165, h=4: 165, h=5: 165; (s3c-ann-net): h=1: 165, h=2: 165, h=3: 165, h=4: 165, h=5: 165.

The effect is the standardised effect of the refit the forecast used times the scale at the anchor in bp. The pooled row is printed at every lead and the regime rows at h = 3 only (the record holds them all). The interval is a bootstrap interval of the mean over decision days, which share refits; it is not a sampling interval for the coefficient. A term enters a fit only when at least three training pairs carry it; the share of days in the fit is shown for the bill and the coupon term (a term of the other leg in a leg-reference walk is not in its fit at h = 2 to 5 and reads 0 there).

Groups of fewer than 20 days, listed and not tested: month/2026-02 (19), month/2026-09 (3), pressure_day_type/quarter_end_turn (4), pressure_day_type/tax_date (6), pressure_day_type/turn (12), pressure_day_type/year_end (1), settlement_day_type/h=2: bill settlement day with a cash management bill (1), settlement_day_type/h=3: bill settlement day with a cash management bill (1), settlement_day_type/h=4: bill settlement day with a cash management bill (1), settlement_day_type/h=5: bill settlement day with a cash management bill (1), target_day_type/fomc_day (10), target_day_type/month_end (16), target_day_type/quarter_end (2), target_day_type/tax_date (6).

## Questions for Eleonora (marked; defaults change no claim)

**Question 1. The announcement dating.** These arms read `metadata/settlement_announcement_dating.json`, which is measured and off. Turning it on in the published declaration is her decision, on `docs/decisions/information-set.md` and on `metadata/sources.json`, which the final-test pre-registration freezes. Nothing here asks for it, and no result on this page is a reason to.

**Options** (the default applies if you have not answered by the next 5 pm batch):

1. **Leave the dating off** (default). Nothing changes.
2. **Ask for a prospective test of the dating**, in a directive of its own, on the confirmation days only.

**Question 2. The bill leg.** The 12:00 announcement time of a cash management bill is unverified (Lens E, item G). Should the bill leg be dropped, or read only for the regular bills, if the dating is ever used?

**Options** (the default applies if you have not answered by the next 5 pm batch):

1. **Keep both legs and the caveat** (default).
2. **Read the coupon leg alone** until the clock time of a cash management bill announcement can be verified.

**Question 3. What follows from this result.** (s3c) stays the H5 candidate. Whether either arm replaces it rests on days already seen.

**Options** (the default applies if you have not answered by the next 5 pm batch):

1. **(s3c) stays the candidate and nothing changes** (default).
2. **Draft a directive that tests the better arm prospectively**, on the confirmation days only, under the scoring protocol.
